FIRST PART. 

PRAYERS. 

I. 

BEGINNING PRAYERS. 

In the name of the Father, and of the Son, and of the Holy Ghost. Amen.

By the prayers of our holy fathers, O Lord, Jesus Christ, our God, have mercy on us.

Glory to Thee, our God, glory to 

Thee. 

Prayer to the Holy Ghost. 

O Heavenly King, Comforter, Spirit of truth, Who art everywhere and fillest all things, Treasury of blessings and Giver of life; come and dwell within us and cleanse us of all stain, and save, O gracious Lord, our souls.

PERVA ČASŤ. 

MOLITVY. 

I. 

NAČALNII MOLITVY. 

Vo imja Otca, i Syna, i Svjataho Ducha. Amiň.

Molitvami svjatych Otec našich, Hospodi Isuse Christe Bože naš, pomiluj nas. 

Slava tebi Bože naš, slava tebi. 

Molitva k Svjatomu Duchu. 

Carju nebesnyj Utišiteľu, Duše istiny, vezdi syj, i vsja ispolňajaj; sokrovisče blahich, i žizni podateľu, prijdi i vselisja v ny, i očisti ny ot vsjakija skverni, i spasi blaže, duši naša.

Beginning Prayers Prayers to the Holy Trinity. 

Holy God, Holy Mighty One, Holy Immortal One, have mercy on us. (3.)

Glory be to the Father, and the Son, and the Holy Ghost, now and ever, and forever and ever. Amen. 

O Most Holy Trinity, have mercy on us; O Lord, cleanse us of our sins; O Master, forgive us our transgressions; O Holy One, visit us and heal our infirmities for Thy name's sake. 

Lord, have mercy. (3.) now and ever: 

The Lord's Prayer. 

Glory: 

Our Father, Who art in heaven, hallowed be Thy name; Thy Kingdom come; Thy will be done on earth, as it is in heaven. Give us this day our daily bread; and forgive us our trespasses, as we forgive those who trespass against us; and lead us not into temptation, but deliver us from evil.

Načalnii Molitvy 2 Molitvy k Trojci Svjatoj.

Svjatyj Bože, svjatyj kripkij, svjatyj bezsmertnyj, pomiluj nas. (3.) 

Slava Otcu, i Synu, i Svjatomu 

Duchu, i nyňi i prisno, i vo viki vikov. Amiň. 

Presvjataja Trojce, pomiluj nas; 

Hospodi, očisti hrichi naša; Vladyko, prosti bezzakonija naša; Svjatyj, positi i iscili nemosči naša, imene tvojeho radi. 

Hospodi pomiluj. (3.) — Slava: i nyňi: 

Molitva Hospodňa. 

Otče naš, iže jesi na nebesich, da svjatitsja imja tvoje. Da prijdet cartvije tvoje. Da budet voľa tvoja, jako na nebesi i na zemli.

Chlib naš nasusčnyj dažď nam dnes', i ostavi nam dolhi naša, jakože i my ostavľajem dolžnikom našim. I ne vvedi nas VO isku- šenije, no izbavi nas ot lukavaho.

Beginning Prayers 

For Thine is the kingdom, and the power, and the glory of the Father, and the Son, and the Holy 

Ghost, now and ever, and forever and ever. Amen. 

Lord, have mercy. (3.) Glory: now and ever: 

Adoration to Christ, Our King. 

Come, let us adore the King, our God. 

Come, let us adore Christ, our 

King and God. 

Come, let us adore and worship the Lord, Jesus Christ, our King and God.

Psalm 50. 

Have mercy on me, O God, according to Thy great mercy, and according to the multitude of Thy tender mercies blot out my iniquity. 

Wash me yet more from my iniquity, and cleanse me from my sin.

For I know my iniquity, and my sin is always before me.

Načalnii Molitvy 3 Jako tvoje jesť carstvo i sila i slava, Otca, i Syna, i Svjataho 

Ducha, nyňi i prisno, i vo vikov. Amiň. 

Hospodi pomiluj. (12.) i nyňi: viki 

Slava: 

Poklonenije Christu Carju. 

Priidite poklonimsja Carevi našemu Bohu. 

Priidite poklonimsja Christu Carevu našemu Bohu.

Priidite poklonimsja, i pripadem samomu Hospodu Isusu 

Carevi i Bohu našemu. 

Psalom 50. Christu, Pomiluj mja Bože, po velicij milosti tvojej, i po množestvu sčedrot tyoich očisti bezzakonije moje.

Najpače omyj mja ot bezzakonija mojeho, i ot hricha mojeho očisti mja. 

Jako bezzakonije moje az znaju, i hrich moj predomnoju jesť vynu.

Beginning Prayers 

To Thee only have I sinned, and have done evil before Thee; that 

Thou mayst be justified in Thy words, and mayst overcome when 

Thou are judged.

For behold I was conceived in iniquities; and in sins did my mother conceive me. 

For behold Thou hast loved truth; the uncertain and hidden things of 

Thy wisdom Thou hast made manifest to me. 

Thou shalt sprinkle me with hyssop, and I shall be cleansed; Thou shalt wash me, and I shall be made whiter than snow. 

To my hearing Thou shalt give joy and gladness; and the bones that have been humbled shall rejoice. 

Turn away Thy face from my sins, and blot out all my iniquities. 

Create a clean heart in me, O 

God; and renew a right spirit within my bowels. 

Cast me not away from Thy face; and take not Thy holy spirit from me.

Načalnii Molitvy Tebi jedinomu sohrišich, i lukavoje pred toboju sotvorich, jako da opravdišisja v slovesich tvoich, i pobidiši vnehda suditi ti. 

Se bo v bezzakoniich začat jesm, i v hrisich rodi mja mati moja. 

Se bo istinu vozľubil jesi; bezvistnaja i tajnaja premudrosti tvojeja javil mi jesi.

Okropiši mja hissopom, i očisčusja; omyješi mja, pače sňiha ubilusja.

Sluchu mojemu dasi radosť i veselije; vozradujutsja kosti smirennyja. mo- Otvrati lice tvoje ot hrich ich, i vsja bezzakonija moja očisti. 

Serdce čisto soziždi v mňi Bože, i duch prav obnovi v utrobi mojej. 

Ne otverži mene ot lica tvojeho, i Ducha tvojeho svjataho ne otimi ot mene.

Beginning Prayers 

Restore unto me the joy of Thy salvation; and strengthen me with a perfect spirit. 

I will teach the unjust Thy ways; and the wicked shall be converted to Thee. 

Deliver me from blood, O God, Thou God of my salvation; and my tongue shall extol Thy justice. 

O Lord, Thou wilt open my lips; and my mouth shall declare Thy praise. 

For if Thou hadst desired sacrifice,

I would indeed have given it; with burnt offerings Thou wilt not be delighted. 

A sacrifice to God is an afflicted spirit; a contrite and humbled heart, O God, Thou wilt not despise. 

Deal favorably, O Lord, in Thy good will with Sion; that the walls of Jerusalem may be built up. 

Then shalt Thou accept the sacrifice of justice, oblations and whole burnt offerings; then shall they lay calves upon Thy altar.

Načalnii Molitvy 5 Vozdažď mi radosť spasenija tvojeho, i duchom vladyčnym utverdi mja. 

Nauču bezzakonnyja putem tvoim, i nečestivii k tebi obrjaťatsja. 

Izbavi mja ot krovej Bože, Bože spasenija mojeho, i vozradujetsja jazyk moj pravdi tvojej.

Hospodi, ustňi moi otverzeši, i usta moja vozvisťat chvalu tvoju.

Jako asče by voschotil jesi žertvy, dal bych ubo, vsesožžennija blahovoliši. ne 

Žertva Bohu Duch sokrušen, serdca sokrušenna i smirenna Boh ne uničižit. 

Ublaži Hospodi blahoslovenijem tvoim Siona, i da soziždutsja stiny Jerusalimskija.

Tohda blahovoliši žertvu pravdy, voznošenije, i vsesožehajemaja, tohda vozložat na oltar tvoj telcy.

Beginning Prayers The Symbol of Faith. 

I believe in one God, Father almighty, maker of heaven and earth, and of all things visible and invisible. And in one Lord Jesus 

Christ, the only-begotten Son of 

God, born of the Father before all ages. Light of light, true God of true God; begotten, not made; consubstantial with the Father, by 

Whom all things were made. Who for us men, and for our salvation, came down from heaven and was incarnate of the Holy Ghost and the Virgin Mary, and was made man. He was crucified also for us under Pontius Pilate, and suffered, and was buried. And the third day

He arose again, according to the 

Scriptures. And ascended into heaven; and He sitteth at the right hand of the Father; and He shall come again with glory to judge the living and the dead; and His kingdom shall have no end. And in the Holy 

Ghost, the Lord and Giver of life, Who proceedeth from the Father

Načalnii Molitvy 

Ispovidanije viry. 

Viruju v jedinaho Boha, Otca vsederžiteľa, tvorca neba i zemli, vidimych, že vsich i nevidimych. I v jedinaho Hospoda Isusa Christa,

Syna Božija jedinorodnaho, ot Otca roždennaho prežde vsich vik. Svita ot svita, Boha istinna ot Boha istinna, roždenna, ne sotvorenna, jedinosusčna Otcu imže vsja byša. 

Nas dila čelovik, i našeho radi spasenija, sšedšaho s nebes, i voplotivšahosja ot Ducha svjata, i Marii Divy, i vočelovičšasja. Raspjataho že za ny pri Pontijstim Pilati, stradavšaho i pohrebena, i voskresšaho v tretij deň po pisanijem. I vozšedšaho na nebesa, i sidasčaho odesnuju Otca, i paki hrjadusčaho so slavoju suditi živym i mertvym, jehože carstviju ne budet konca. I Vo Ducha 

Svjataho, Hospoda životvorjasčaho, iže ot Otca i Syna ischoďasčaho. 

Iže s Otcem i so Synom spoklaňajema, i soslavima, hlaholavšaho

Beginning Prayers and the Son, Who together with the Father and the Son is adored and glorified, Who spoke by the Prophets. In one, holy, catholic and apostolic Church. I confess one baptism for the remission of sins. 

I expect the resurrection of the dead. And the life of the age to Amen. come.

Prayer of Penance. 

Remit, pardon and forgive, O God, our sins committed consciously, or unconsciously, by word, or deed, by thought, or purpose, by day, night. Forgive all these, for Thou art gracious and lover of mankind.

The Angelic Salutation. 

or 

Hail, Mother of God, Virgin Mary, full of grace, the Lord is with thee; blessed are thou amongst women, and blessed is the fruit of thy womb. 

For thou hast borne Christ, the 

Saviour and Deliverer of our souls.

Načalnii Molitvy 7 Proroki. V jedinu, svjatuju, sobor nuju i Apostolskuju Cerkov. Ispo viduju jedino kresčenije v ostavlenije hrichov. Čaju voskresenije mertvych. I žizni budusčaho vika.

Amiň. 

Molitva pokajanija.

Oslabi, ostavi, otpusti Bože sohrišenija naša, volnaja i nevolnaja, jaže v slovi i v ďili, jaže vo vidinii i nevidinii, jaže v umi i v pomyšlenii, jaže v dni i v nosči, vsja nam prosti, jako blah i čelovikoľubec. 

Anhelskij privit.

Bohorodice Ďivo, radujsja obradovannaja Marije, Hospod s toboju. 

Blahoslovenna ty v ženach, i blahosloven plod čreva tvojeho; jako rodila jesi Christa, Spasa, Izbaviteľa dušam našim.

Beginning Prayers Prayers to Blessed Virgin Mary. 

It is truly meet and just to glorify thee, ever blessed and immaculate 

Mother of our God. Who art more honourable than the Cherubim and incomparably more glorious than the

Seraphim, who hast borne without stain God, the Word; thee, O 

Mother of God, we magnify. 

We fly to thy patronage, O Virgin 

Mother of God. Despise not our prayers in our necessities, but who art alone pure and blessed, deliver us from all danger. 

O most glorious ever Virgin Mary, Mother of Christ, our God, receive our prayers and transmit them to thy Son and our God, that He, for thy sake, enlighten and save our souls.

Prayers to the Angels and Saints. 

All ye heavenly powers, holy angels and archangels, beseech God for us sinners. 

O, holy and glorious Apostles, Prophets, Martyrs and all Saints, beseech God for us sinners.

Načalnii Molitvy 8 Molitvy k Bohorodici.

Dostojno jest jako voistinnu, blažiti ťa Bohorodicu, prisnoblažen nuju i preneporočnuju i Mater' 

Boha našeho, čestňijšuju Cheruvim, i slavňijšuju bez sravnenija Serafim, bez ist linija Boha Slova roždšuju, susčuju Bohorodicu ta veličajem. 

Pod tvoju milosť pribihajem Bohorodice Divo, molitv našich ne prezri v skorbech, no ot bid izbavi nas jedina čistaja i blahoslovennaja. 

Preslavnaja, prisnodivo Marije, Mati Christa Boha našeho, prijmi molitvy naša, i donesi ja Synu tvojemu, i Bohu našemu, da spaset i prosvitit tebe radi duši naša.

Molitvy k Anhelam i Svjatym. 

Vsja nebesnyja sily, svjatii Anhely i Archanhely, molite Boha o nas hrišnych.

Svjatii, slavnii i vsechvaľnii Apostoly, Prorocy, Mučenicy i vsi Svjatii, molite Boha o nas hrišnych.

Evening Prayers Prayer of the Publican. 

God, have mercy on me, a sinner. 

God, cleanse me of my sins and have mercy on me. 

I have sinned without number, forgive me, O Lord.

Adoration to the Holy Trinity. 

The Father is our Hope, the Son our Refuge, the Holy Ghost our 

Protector; Most Holy Trinity, our 

God, glory to Thee.

In the name of the Father, and of the Son, and of the Holy Ghost. Amen.

II. 

EVENING PRAYERS. 

(After the usual Beginning Prayers.) Tropar. 

Have mercy on us, O Lord, have mercy on us, for being at a loss what to say, we offer this prayer to 

Thee, our Master, have mercy on us. 

Glory be to the Father and the 

Son and the Holy Ghost.

Večernii Molitvy 

Molitva mytareva. 

Bože, milostiv budi mňi hrišnomu.

A prayer for the Priesthood. on 

Lord, Jesus Christ, shepherd of all the faithful, I humbly beseech

Thee to behold Thy successor earth, the Pope of Rome. Bless his endeavors and deign to grant desires for the welfare of the 

Church. Humble all his foes. Give to him strength and health, that he may govern in peace the fold confided to his care and lead it to the heavenly kingdom. Amen.

Prayer of the unmarried. 

My God, by whose grace I am able to distinguish the good from the evil, do not allow that I may act against Thy Commandments and that I may sin against the holy purity, whether by thought, word or action. Especially guard me, my Lord, when passions overwhelm me and tempt me against 

Thy holy Commandment. Grant, my Lord, strength against the enemies of my salvation and protect me, that with my age, I may be daily more wise and virtuous. I ask thee especially, most pure Virgin Mary and purest Saint Joseph, intercede for me that grace, that

I may follow Thee in the virtue of shining purity. Amen.

A prayer of an orphan. 

O most merciful God, Thou preservest the substance of all creatures, by Thy most gracious providence every creature lives and flourishes. 

Thou dost not forsake even the smallest worm, but providest for and feedest it. I humbly beseech Thee, do not withdraw Thine aid from me. 

Through Thine untraceable decree my father and mother departed from me and I am, bereft of their parental care and protection. But do Thou, O good Father, take me under Thy shelter and be my protector throughout my whole life. Console my afflicted heart by Thy care and strengthen me by patience in need.

Various Prayers 

Awaken in the hearts of my relatives and friends compassion and pity for me, that they give me a helping hand, instruct me in deeds pleasing to Thee and lead me on the path of Thy commandments. Avert all temptations from me, that I may never be tempted by worldly vanity and inclined toward wickedness.

Destroy within me all evil intentions and desires, that I never forsake Thy goodness and love. Plant love of work into my heart and be my relief in all things. Preserve me from affliction, misfortune, harm and suffering. O most compassionate, loving God, bless my protectors and instructors and all my benefactors. 

Grant my departed parents remission of all their sins; look down upon them with pity and admit them to the eternal joys of Paradise. For

Thine is the property of loving and saving us, O Lord, and we return Thee praise, Father, and Son, and Holy Ghost, now and ever, and forever and ever. Amen.

V. 

PRAYERS DURING LOW MASS. 

At the beginning of Holy Mass. 

O Lord, God, we, Thy unworthy servants have come to Thy holy church to return Thee thanks, to meditate upon Thine almighty power and also upon Thine infinite love toward us, the ineffable humility of Thine only-begotten Son, Jesus

Christ, His voluntary sacrifice upon the cross, the donation by Him of 

His most holy Body and Blood, His terrible passion and death, and 

His glorious resurrection. O Lord, receive our prayers which we intend to offer Thee during this holy 

Sacrifice; accept our burning hearts, give ear to our pleas and grant our souls and bodies peace.

SECOND PART. 

HOLY CONFESSION AND COMMUNION. 

I. 

DEVOTIONS FOR HOLY CONFESSION. 

Instructions. 

God does not desire the death of a sinner, but that he repent his sins and live. For this reason he has provided for us, in the Holy Sacrament of Penance, the means whereby we may gain forgiveness of our sins and be again restored to the favor of our heavenly Father, which we had forfeited by our sins. 

When coming to confession prepare yourself thoroughly. Beseech 

God to give you grace to make a good examination of conscience and the strength to make a sincere confession and to amend your life.

DRUHA ČASŤ. 

SVJATA SPOVIĎ I PRIČASTIJE. 

I. 

NABOŽNOSTI SVJATOJ 

SPOVIDI. 

Korotkoje nastavlenije. 

Boh ne chočet smerti hrišnika, no čtoby on pokajalsja i žil. Iz sej pričiny postaralsja On O nas vo Tajňi Pokajanija; a to so tym želanijem, čtoby my tamo mohli dostati otpusčenije hrichov našich i nanovo vozobnoviti blahosklonnosť našeho nebesnaho Otca, kotoryj otlišil nas za naši hrichi. 

Preduhotovi sebe sovistno na 

Spovid. Prosi Boha na to, čtoby dal tebi lasku zrobiti dobryj ispyt sovisti, i silu iskrenno vyspovidatisja i polipšati svoj život.

Begin your examination with your last confession. Meditate whether a grievous sin was not forgotten through want of proper examination, or concealed through shame; whether you confessed with true sorrow and purpose of amendment; whether you have performed your penance in a proper spirit.

Then examine yourself on the different sins committed against the Ten Commandments, the Commandments of the Church, etc. Go over these carefully and note in what instance you violated them. Do not neglect to consider the circumstances which alter the grievousness of the sin, nor the various ways we become accessory to the sins of others.

EXCERPTS FROM THE CATHECHISM. 

The Ten Commandments of God. 

I. I am the Lord, thy God; 

Thou shalt not have strange gods before me.

Načinaj ispyt sovisti so posl'idnoju tvojeju spovideju. Ci ty ne zabyl dajakij važňijšij hrich pro nedostatka ispyta, abo ci ty ne zatajil dačto iz haňby? City ne spovidalsja bez istinnaho žaľu, i bez namirenija sebe poľipšati? Ci ty otkončil svoju pokutu točno?

Potomu ispytuj sebe o raznych hrichach, na osnovi desjať zapovidej Božiich; na osnovi zapovidej cerkovnych; na osnovi holovnych hrichov, i tak daľše. Peresmotri ti hrichi vnimateľno i zamerkuj: jakim obrazom perestupil ty toty? Ne propusti pozerati i obstojateľstva hrichov, kotory peremiňajut ich vahu; takže i razny dorohi, so kotorymi stalisja my součastnikami vo hrichach druhich.

EXAMINATION OF CONSCIENCE. 

Preparatory Questions. 

When did you make your last confession? Did you take sufficient pains to awaken sorrow for your sins? Did you intentionally omit any mortal sin? Did you forget to confess any mortal sin? Did you neglect to do your penance? Have you carried out the resolutions made at your last confession to amend your life? Have you repaired the evil done to your neighbor? Etc. 

Examination on the Ten

Commandments. 

I. Have you had doubt in matters of faith? Murmured against God in adversity? Despaired of His mercy?

Poslidny riči. 

1. Smert. 2. Sud. 3. Peklo vič

noje. 4. Carstvo nebesnoje.

ISPYT SOVISTI. 

Predvariteľny voprosy. 

Koli spovidalsja ja posľidnyj raz? 

Ci usilovalsja ja tohdy vozbuždati žaľ za hrichi moi? Ci ja narokom ne zatajil, abo ci ja ne zabyl vo spovidi vypovisti dajakij smerteľnyj hrich? Ci ja otkončil mňi podannu pokutu? Ci polipšal ja svoj život ot poslidnoj mojej spovidi, jak ja to tohdy rišil? Ci ja vynahorodil škodu mnoju dilannu bližnemu mojemu? I tak daľše!

PRAYERS BEFORE CONFESSION. 

1. most 

O Lord God, my holy Father, I am unworthy to be called Thy son;

I have transgressed Thy holy laws and wasted the portion of substance received from, Thee. Thou, amiable 

Father, hast granted me an immortal soul, strong body, clear mind and healthy limbs; and I, an ingrate, although redeemed by the precious Blood of Jesus, although numerously regenerated in spirit by the Sacraments, have, nevertheless, departed from the path of Thy commandments, and by living riotously and spurning all Thy gifts, have again angered Thee. O merciful 

Father! I am no longer worthy to be called Thy son, I dare not raise my eyes to Thy majesty; I blush, for I have not fulfilled Thy will. 

O my Lord, God, what shall I do? 

Where shall I hide before Thy wrath? Thou seest everything. Thou

1. 

Hospodi Bože, svjatyj moj 

Otče! Ja nedostojnyj zvatisja synom tvoim, bo ja perestupil tvoi svjaty zakony, i opustošil časť ot 

Tebe polučennaho imusčestva. O ľubeznyj Otče! Ty dal jesi mňi bezsmertnu dušu, siľnoje tilo, čistyj razum i zdorovy členy; i ja neblahodarnyj, choťaj otkuplennyj byl jesm najdorohšoju kroviju Isusa, choťaj mnohokrat obnovlenije dostal jesm vo mojej duši čerez Sv. Tajny, odnakož nanovo i samovoľno otyjšol ja iz dorohi zapovidej tvoich, i razvratno živše otverhl ja vsi tvoi dary i nanovo prohňival ja Tebe. 

O miloserdnyj Otče! Ja daľše ne dostoin zvatisja tvoim synom; i ne smiju vozdvihnuti oči moi ko tvojemu veličestvu, bo ja stydajusja, čto ne ispolnil tvoju voľu. O 

Hospodi Bože moj! Ta čto dilati

seest the enormity of my sins, knowest the condition of my soul.

I will, however, arise and trusting in Thy mercy, cry to Thee: Father, I have sinned against heaven and before Thee, I am not worthy to be called Thy son, no longer deserving of Thy mercy, but do Thou, Who art infinitely merciful, have compassion on me and once receive me, no longer as Thy son, but as one of Thy servants. more 

Look down upon me now as Thou hast upon the penitent and prodigal son, forgive my levity, forgive my transgressions and mercifully have pity on me. Do not clothe me in lustrous robes, only grant me Thy grace and remission of my sins. 

Help me, O merciful Lord God, that

I contritely beg for Thy forgiveness and never again offend and anger

Thee. Amen.

PRAYERS AT CONFESSION. 

with 

Approach the confessional the same reverence as would fill your heart if Christ, our Lord, were seated there in person ready to hear your confession, for the priest is really the representative of Christ. When you kneel down commence your confession the following prayer: with 

I, a sinner, confess to Almighty 

God, One in the Holy Trinity, to the Blessed Virgin Mary, Mother of God, to all saints, and to you, my father confessor, all my sins. 

Then tell your father confessor when you made your last confession and begin the avowal of your sins. Confess all your sins with an humble heart, and conclude with the following prayer:

For these and all my other sins, which I remember, or cannot call to mind, I am heartily sorry that 

I have offended my good God and angered Him against me. I am bitterly repentant and promise with

PRAYER AFTER CONFESSION. 

O God of mercy, having now through Thy gracious goodness unburdened my conscience of the guilt with which it was oppressed, and, in the humblest manner I was able, revealed all the sins I could recollect to Thy minister, I humbly beseech

Thee to accept this confession and forgive me all my trespasses, those which I have forgotten, as well as those I have remembered. Grant me grace, O Lord, to live more carefully hereafter and to refrain from my former vices, which I utterly detest.

I firmly resolve never to be guilty of them in the future, but especially, most bountiful and merciful Saviour, enable me to withstand those temptations, with which I am most in-

MOLITVA PO SPOVIĎI. 

O Hospodi miloserdija! Iz tvojej laskavoj dobroty teper' oblehšenna jesť sovisť moja ot hrichov, kotory davili mene. Ja tvojemu sluhovi-svjasčenniku, so jakim liše smirennym sposobom mohol jesm, otkryl vsi hrichi moi. Pokorno umolaju Tebe: prijmi siju spovid moju i otpusti mňi vsi hrichi moi; tak toty, jaki ja zabyl, jak i ti, na jaki ja pamjatal. O Hospodi!

Podaj mni ostorožňijše žiti potomu, i vozderžatisja ot davnych moich chib, kotory ja uže cilkom nenavidžu. Ja tverdo rišaju: so nimi nikoli ne hrišiti; no, o najsčedrotlivijšij i najmiloserdňijšij Spasiteľu moj, zďilaj možlivomu, čtoby

fested, and to avoid all occasions for offending Thee again. If a just man falls seven times in the day, how much more reason have I to fear for myself, O Lord, and dread that 

I shall not be steadfast in my resolutions, having through my own frailty and vicious customs increased the natural blindness and weakness in which I was born. Yet, O Lord,

I firmly resolve, through Thy merciful assistance, never to consent to any mortal sin, from which I humbly beseech Thee to preserve me while 

I live; as to my venial sins and imperfections, I resolve to strive against them and hope, through Thy goodness, at length to amend them. Amen.

AN INDULGENCED PRAYER. 

(Everyone, after having been to Confession and having received Holy Communion, contritely saying the following prayers before a cross or a picture depicting the crucified Jesus, and praying for the intention of the Holy Father, may gain plenary indulgence. This may also be applied to the souls in Purgatory.) 

Dear sweet Jesus! I prostrate myself before Thee and with fervent zeal of my soul beseech Thee, transplant into my heart the faculties of faith, hope and charity; true sorrow for my sins and a strong will to amend my life now, when 

I think, with great emotion and affliction of Thy five wounds, keeping before my eyes, dear Jesus,

A prayer to the most holy 

Mother of God. 

O most holy Mother of God, light of my darkened soul, my hope, protection, refuge, comfort and joy. 

I thank thee that thou hast enabled me, who am unworthy, to be participant of the most pure Body and most precious Blood of thy Son. 

Thou, who hast borne the true 

Light, enlighten the eyes of my heart. Thou who didst bear the 

Fountain of immortality, enliven me, who am darkened by sin. As the most loving Mother of merciful God, do thou have mercy on me and grant me a repentant and contrite heart, and vouchsafe that to my last breath I may receive uncondemned the sanctification of the most pure 

Sacrament for the healing of my body and soul. And give me tears of repentance and thanksgiving that 

I may praise and glorify thee throughout all the days of my life, for thou art blessed and glorified forever. Amen.

Molitva k Presvjatoj Bohorodici. 

O presvjataja Mati Boha, svitloste zatemnitoj mojej duši; nadeždo, pokrove, pribižisče, uťišenije i radovanije moje! Blahodarju, čto ty spodobila mene nedostojnaho učastnikom byti prečistaho Tila i prečestnoj Krovi Syna tvojeho. Ty, kto narodilas' istinnyj Svit, prosviti oči serdca mojeho. Ty, kto narodilas' istočnika bezsmertija, oživotvori mene umersčvlennaho hrichom. 

Jako preľubezna Mati miloserdnaho

Boha, pomiluj mja, i dažď mňi kajusčoje i sokrušennoje serdce; i pozvoli, čtoby do poslidňaho vozdychanija moh ja prinimati prečistu 

Tajnu vo iscilenije mojeho tila i duši. O daj mňi slezy pokajanija i blahodarenija, čtoby ja moh blahodariti i slaviti tebe vo vsja dni života mojeho, jako blahoslovenna i preproslavlenna jesi vo viki vikov. Amiň.

THE GOSPELS. 

(From Saint Matthew; section 43.) 

The Lord saith to His disciples:

All things are delivered to me by my Father, and no one knoweth the Son, but the Father, neither doth any one know the Father, but the Son, and he to whom it shall please the Son to reveal Him. 

Come to me all ye that labour and are burdened, and I will refresh you. Take up my yoke upon you, and learn of me, because I am meek and humble of heart. And you shall find rest to your souls. 

For my yoke is sweet and my burden light.

I am the good shepherd. The good shepherd giveth his life for his sheep. But the hireling, and he that is not the shepherd, whose own the sheep are not, seeth the wolf coming, and leaveth the sheep and flieth; and the wolf catcheth and scattereth the sheep. Tropar. 

We humble sinners now run to the Mother of God and in repentance prostrate ourselves, crying: O 

Queen, mercifully help us; hurry, for we are perishing from the multitude of sins; turn not away from thy vain servants, for we have thee alone as our Patroness.

Glory: Now and ever: 

O Mother of God, we unworthy ones shall never cease to proclaim thy might, for if thou were to cease praying for us, who would deliver us from such misfortunes? 

Who would have preserved us until now in freedom? We shall never depart from thee, O Queen, for thou, who alone art blessed, ever savest thy servants from all calamities.

Thou mayst be justified in Thy words, and mayst overcome when 

Thou are judged.

For behold I was conceived in iniquities; and in sins did my mother conceive me. 

For behold Thou hast loved truth; the uncertain and hidden things of Thy wisdom Thou hast made manifest to me. 

Thou shalt sprinkle me with hyssop, and I shall be cleansed; 

Thou shalt wash me, and I shall be made whiter than snow. 

To my hearing Thou shalt give joy and gladness; and the bones that have been humbled shall rejoice. 

Turn away Thy face from my sins, and blot out all my iniquities.

Jako bezzakonije moje az znaju: i hrich moj predomnoju jesť vynu.

Se bo v bezzakoniich začat jesm: i v hrisich rodi mja mati moja. 

Se bo vo istinu vozľubil jesi: bezvistnaja i tajnaja premudrosti tvojeja javil mi jesi

Okropiši mja hissopom, i očisčusja: omyješi mja, i pače sňiha ubifusja.

Create a clean heart in me, O

God; and renew a right spirit within my bowels. 

Cast me not away from Thy face; and take not Thy holy spirit from me. 

Restore unto me the joy of Thy salvation; and strengthen me with a perfect spirit. 

I will teach the unjust Thy ways; and the wicked shall be converted to Thee. 

Deliver me from blood, O God, Thou God of my salvation; and my tongue shall extol Thy justice. 

O Lord, Thou wilt open my lips; and my mouth shall declare 

Thy praise. 

For if Thou hadst desired sacrifice, I would indeed have given it; with burnt offerings Thou wilt not be delighted.

A sacrifice to God is an afflicted spirit; a contrite and humbled heart, O God, Thou wilt not despise. 

Deal favorably, O Lord, in Thy good will with Sion; that the walls of Jerusalem may be built up.

MOST CANON OF SUPPLICATION IN HONOR OF THE HOLY MOTHER OF GOD. 

Irmos 1. (Tone 8.)

Having crossed the water as dry land and escaped the Egyptian evil, the Israelite cried out: Let us sing to God, our Deliverer. 

Irmos 3. 

O Lord, creator of the heavenly sphere and founder of the Church, do Thou strengthen me in Thy love, haven of desires and strength of the faithful, who alone lovest mankind. 

Irmos 4.

I have heard of the mystery of 

Thy providence, understood Thy works and glorified Thy divinity.

Kondaks. (Tone 6.) 1. 

O unassailable Patroness of Christians, immutable advocate before the Creator, do not spurn the voices of the sinners' prayers, but do, being gracious, come to the aid of us faithfully calling thee: 

Hasten to the prayer, hearken to the supplication, ever interceding, O Mother of God, for those who honor thee.

2. We have no other help, we have no other hope outside of thee, O most pure Virgin; do thou help us, we hope in thee, we glory in thee; we are thy servants, let us not be confounded. Ikos.

Extend thy hands on which, according to the multitude of goodness, thou hast received the Master as an infant; do not forsake us who are always hoping in thee; by thy vigilant prayers and incalculable forgiveness have compassion upon us and grant our souls thy ever shedding mercy; for we sinners have in thee a benefactress against all misfortunes and evil, but having the bounties of forgiveness, hasten to the prayer and hearken to the supplication, ever interceding, O Mother of God, for those who honor thee.

Elizabeth heard the salutation of Mary, the infant leaped in her womb, and Elizabeth was filled with the Holy Ghost; and she cried out with a loud voice, and said: Blessed art thou amongst women and blessed is the fruit of thy womb! And why this honor to me, that the Mother of my 

Lord should come to me? For behold as soon as the voice of thy salutation sounded in my ears, the infant in my womb leaped for joy. And blessed art thou that hast believed, because those things shall be accomplished that were spoken to thee by the Lord. And

Mary said: My soul doth magnify the Lord, and my spirit hath rejoiced in God, my Saviour. Because He hath regarded the humility of His handmaid; for behold from henceforth all generations shall call me blessed. Because

He that is mighty hath done great things to me; and holy is His name. And Mary remained with her about three months; and she returned to her own home.

and salvation of those who hasten to thee; I truly know that I have greatly sinned and angered the benevolent God, begot in flesh by thee; but I have many images of those who before me abused His mercy: publicans, dissolutes and other sinners, who through penance and confession received remission of their sins. Having before the eyes of my sinful soul the images of those pardoned and contemplating upon such mercy of God received by them, I, too, a sinner, venture to have recourse to thy compassion. O most gracious 

Queen, that thou give me a hand of help and procure from thy Son and God the remission of my grave sins. I believe and confess that He 

Whom thou hast borne, thy Son, is truly Christ, Son of the living God, judge of the living and the dead, Who shall render to all according to their deeds. I further believe and confess thou art truly the Mother of God, source mercy, consolation of the afflicted, haven of the lost, powerful of and

tekajusčich k tebi! ceaseless mediatrix, exceedingly loving the human race, and security of my repentance. O most merciful

Lady, humanity truly has no help and refuge outside of thee, and none placing their hope in thee have been shamed and adoring God through thee been forsaken. For this reason I beseech thine incalculable goodness: open the gates of thy mercy to me who have erred and fallen into the depth of a morass. Do not despise me who am vile, do not condemn my supplication, do not forsake me, wretch, who has been grasped for perdition by the malicious enemy; but beseech the merciful God, begotten by thee, for me, that He remit my grievous sins and deliver me from my ruin, so that I, with all recipients of thy mercy, may praise and glorify the mercy of 

God, born of thee, and thy pure intercession for me in this life and in the age to come.

BEHEADING OF ST. JOHN THE BAPTIST. 

Aug. 29 (Sept. 11). 

Tropar. (Tone 2.) O Precursor, the memory of the just is blessed, but for thee the testimony of the 

Lord is sufficient; for thou hast truly shown thyself to be the most honorable of the Prophets, since thou wast held worthy to baptize in the waters Him, Whom thou hast preached. Wherefore thou hast gladly suffered for the truth and announced even to those in hell God appearing in flesh and granting us great mercy.

Kondak. (Tone 5.) The behead ing of the glorious Precursor was a divine dispensation to preach even to those in hell the advent of the Saviour. Therefore, let Herodias lament, for she asked unlawful murder; she did not love the law of 

God, nor living life, but only time's deceitfulness.

IMMACULATE CONCEPTION OF THE BLESSED VIRGIN MARY. 

Note: Is it a praiseworthy custom, that in certain of our parishes the feast of "Immaculate Conception" is solemnly observed; that is, when 

St. Anna conceived without original sin her daughter, Mary. The date of this event is December 9 (22). 

Tropar. (Tone 4.) Today the ties of childlessness have been loosened, for God listened, beyond hope, to 

Joachim and Anna, and promises them that from them will be begotten the daughter of God, from whom will be born the undescribable, becoming man; for this the angels were bidden to sing to her: "Hail, Mary, full of grace, the Lord is with thee!" 

Kondak. (Tone 4.) Today all is rejoicing over the God gives conceiving of Anna, for she is giving birth to the greater than the Word 

God-bearing One.

III. 

TROPARS AND KONDAKS FOR SOME MOVABLE FEASTS. 

PALM SUNDAY. 

Tropar. (Tone 1.) O Christ 

God, before Thy passion Thou hast confirmed the general resurrection, raised Lazarus from the dead; wherefore we also, as children bearing the standard of victory, cry to Thee, the Conqueror of death: Hosanna in the highest, blessed is He that cometh in the name of the Lord. 

Kondak. (Tone 6.) On the throne in heaven and on earth borne by a colt, O Christ God, Thou hast received the praises of the Angels and the hymns of the children crying to Thee: Blessed art Thou Who comest to recall Adam.

Tropar. (Tone 2.) The God fearing Joseph, taking from the cross the most pure body, wrapping it in clean linen, and anointing it with perfumed scents, placed it in a new grave.

Thou art only, Who hast borne the sicknesses and pains of our kind, and as most merciful, do all. can

Različny Tropari 

231 

Pričasten: Blahosloven Boh, iže ne ostavi molitvy mojeja i milosti svojeja ot mene. Alliluia. 4.

O BOĽASČICH. 

Tropar. (4. Hlas.) Skoryj v zastuplenii jedin syj Christe, skoroje svyše pokaži posisčenije, straždusčemu rabu tvojemu (straždusčim rabom tvoim), i izbavi ot neduh, i horkich boľiznej, i vozdvihni ježe piti ta i slaviti neprestanno, molitvami Bohorodicy, jedine čelovikoľubče.

Kondak. (2. Hlas.) Na odri bolizni ležasčaho (ležasčich), i smertnoju ranoju ujazvlennaho, jakože inohda vozdvihl jesi, Spase, Petrovu tesču, i razslablennaho na odri nosimaho, sice i nyňi miloserde, straždusčaho positi i iscili: ty bo jedin jesi neduhi i bolizni roda našeho ponesyj, i vsja mohij, jako mnohomilostiv.

Various Tropars 

5. 

LITURGY FOR THE DEAD. 

Tropar. (Tone 8.) By depth of wisdom Thou, Who alone art Creator, hast benevolently made all things, rest, O Lord, the soul (souls) of Thy servant (servants), for he (she, or they) has (have) placed hope in Thee, Our Creator, Maker and God.

Kondak: Glory be: (Tone 8.) 

Rest with the Saints, O Christ, the soul (souls) of Thy servant (servants), where there is no pain, care, nor anxiety, but eternal life. 

Now and ever: (Tone 8.) We have thee as a bulwark, haven and supplicant before God, to 

Whom thou, O Virgin Mother of 

God, salvation of the faithful, hast given birth.

Tropar: (8. Hlas.) Hlubinoju mudrosti čelovikoľubno vsja strojaj, i poleznoje vsim podavajaj, jedine Soditelu: upokoj Hospodi dušu raba tvojeho (raby tvojeja ili duši rab tvoich); na ťa bo upovanije vozložiša, Tvorca i Žizditeľa, i Boha našeho. 

Kondak; Slava: (8. Hlas.) So svjatymi upokoj Christe dušu raba (raby ili rab) tvojeho (tvojeja ili tvoich), idiže ňisť bolizň, ni pečaľ, ni vozdychanije, no žizň bezkonečnaja. 

Bohorodičen; I nyňi: (8. Hlas.)

Tebi i stinu i pristanisče imamy, i molitvennicu blahoprijatnuju ko Bohu, Jehože rodila jesi, Bohorodice beznevistnaja, virnym spasenije.

Hymn of Thanksgiving 

Paraclete. Thou, Christ, art 

King of glory. Thou art the everlasting Son of the Father. Thou, having taken upon Thee to deliver man, didst not disdain the Virgin's womb. Thou, having overcome the sting of death, hast opened to believers the kingdom of heaven. 

Thou sittest at the right hand of 

God, in the glory of the Father.

Thou, we believe, art the Judge to come. We beseech Thee, therefore, to help Thy servants whom Thou hast redeemed with Thy precious 

Blood. Make them to be numbered with Thy Saints in glory everlasting. O Lord, save Thy people and bless Thine inheritance. And govern them and exalt them forever. 

Day by day we bless Thee, and we praise Thy name forever and ever. Vouchsafe, O Lord, this day to keep us without sin. Have mercy on us, O Lord, have mercy on us. Let Thy mercy, O Lord, be upon us, like as we have hoped in Thee. In Thee, O Lord, I have hoped, let me not be confounded forever. Amen.

Boha sidiši vo slavi Otčej, sudija virišisja priiti. Tebe ubo prosim, pomozi rabom tvoim, ichže čestnoju kroviju iskupil jesi; spodobi so Svjatymi tvoimi, v vičnoj slavi tvojej carstvovati. Spasi ludi tvoja Hospodi, i blahoslovi dostojanije tvoje, i ispravi ja, i voznesi ich vo viki, vo vsja dni blahoslovim tebe, i voschvalim Imja tvoje vo vik, i v vik vika. Na ta Hospodi upovachom, da ne postydimsja vo viki. Amiň.

APPENDIX. 

I. 

DEVOTION OF THE WAY OF THE CROSS. 

BEGINNING PRAYER. 

My crucified Saviour! Lo, I intend to meditate upon Thy passion and death, that I may thank Thee for the enormous goodness of salvation, that I may beseech Thee for my sins and that I may beg the grace of enduring in goodness. 

At the same time, O my Jesus, I offer up these, my meditations and prayers, asking for the remission of the punishment of all the souls in Purgatory, and especially (here mention the name of the soul of whom you think). 

Most merciful Jesus, give me 

Thy powerful grace that I may begin and finish this "Way of the 

Cross" for Thy glory and for the need of my soul. Amen.

DODATOK. 

I. 

NABOŽNOSŤ SVJATOJ KRESTNOJ DOROHI. 

MOLITVA VSTUPNA. 

Spasiteľu moj, na kresti razpjatyj! Se ja choču rozvažati o 

Tvoich mukach i smerti, čtoby Tebi poďakovati za prevelikoje dobrodijstvo spasenija, čtoby Tebe pereprositi za svoi hrichi, ta vyprositi sebi lasku vytervanija vo dobrom. Rovnočasno žertvuju 

Tebi, Isuse, sii moi rozvažanija i molitvy na uprošenije vsim dušam vo čistilisči prosčenija kar, a osoblivo duši (tut spominaj, kotoru dušu maješ na hadci). Isuse,

Učiňi, najmiloserdňijšij svojeju mohučoju laskoju, čtoby ja siju "krestnu dorohu" začal i skončil na Tvoju slavu i na chosen mojej duši. Amiň.

CHRISTE CARJU... 

1. 

Christe Carju spravedlivyj, I Hospodi terpezlivyj, Strašno bityj, katovannyj, I skaridno poruhannyj. 2.

Koľ molilsja v vertohradi, Znajusčij o zlobnoj zradi, 

Čto učenik tvoj lukavyj, Predasť Tebe cilovavyj. 3. 

Na molitvi Ty umľival, I krovavyj pot izlival, O, Ty prijal čašu strasti, Chotil vsich hrišnikov spasti. 4. 

Chot strašalsja ťažkoj strasti,

No vručil vsja Otčej vlasti, Choť jak ťažka učasť tvoja, Rek: da budet voľa tvoja.

JESUS CONDEMNE PTO DEATH

1. 

JESUS IS CONDEMNED TO DEATH. 

Glory, honor and praise to Thee, my most merciful Saviour, because 

Thou, from Thine infinite love for me, a sinner, innocently hast volunteered to take the condemnation for Thy awful death. Most sweet 

Jesus! When I shall stand before 

Thy terrible judgment, be not my 

Judge, but my Saviour. I firmly resolve, that I shall not judge nor condemn anyone; but shall, without mercy, accuse myself in the Sacrament of Penance. My Jesus, be merciful to me! Mary! intercede for me before Jesus on my last judgment.

Our Father: Hail, Mother of God: 

Suffering passion for us, Son of 

God, have mercy on me!

1. 

ISUS OSUŽDENNYJ NA 

SMERŤ. 

Slava, česť i poklon Tebi, najmiloserdňijšij Spasiteľu, za to, čto Ty dla bezkonečnoj svojej ľubvi zhľadom mene hrišnoho izvolil bezvinno priňati na Sebe strašnoje zasuždenije smerti! Najsolodšij moj Isuse! Jak ja stanu na strašnom sudi, ne buď mňi tohda Sudijeju, ale Spasitelem. Ja kripko postanovľaju, čto ne budu nikoho osuždovati ani oproverhovati, no bezposčadno budu obviňovati samoho sebe vo svjatoj Tajňi Pokajanija. Isuse, bud dľa mene miloserdnym! Marije, zastupaj mene pered Isusom na strašnom sudi.

Otče naš.... Bohorodice Ďivo... 

Preterpivyj za nas strasti, Isuse 

Christe, Syne Božij, pomiluj mene!

5. To jesť sovit predvičnaho Boha, da spaset hrišnaho, Proto Sebe bezupornyj, Dal na strast Ahnec pokornyj. 6. 

Tak Hospoda svita seho, I Stvoriteľa svojeho,

Zlostivyi poimali, Jak zlodija povjazali. 7. 

Priveli Jeho v dvor Anny, I tam sluha okajannyj, Udaril v svjatu lanitu,

Po diavolu sovitu. 8. 

Vslid Kajafi predstavili, I ves' narod naustili,

Sej stal zlostno rizy derti, Rek: on povinen jesť smerti.

JESUS MADE TO BEAR HIS CROSS

2. 

JESUS TAKES THE CROSS. 

If I shall not love the Cross, I cannot be the friend of Jesus.

The heavenly way, this is the way of the Cross. My Jesus! forgive my impatience and my murmurings in sufferings and unpleasantness. 

O Lord, let Thy holy will be done on me always! 

Mary, most suffering Mother! intercede for me for that grace, that I would love the Cross and in all be resigned to the divine will.

Our Father: Hail, Mother of God: 

Suffering passion for us, Son of 

God, have mercy on me!

2. 

ISUS BERET NA SEBE KREST. 

Jak ne budu lubiti kresta, ne mohu stati prijatelem Isusa. Doroha neba, se jest doroha krestna. 

Isuse, prosti mňi moi neterpezlivosti i narikaňa VO terpinijach i prikrosťach! Hospodi, naj na mňi vsehda spolnitsja svjata Tvoja voľa! 

Marije, mati najterpezlivijša, vyprosi mňi totu lasku, čtoby ja po- Tubil krest i vo vsem zhodilsja so svjatoju voleju Božijeju. 

Otče naš... Bohorodice Ďivo... 

Preterpivyj za nas strasti, Isuse 

Christe, Syne Božij, pomiluj mene!

9. Voiny bijut, ukarjajut, Hlumlatsja, i voprošajut:

Proroci nam kto ťa bijet? 

Ves' narod pakosti dijet. 10. 

Rano ves' narod sobralsja, Christa ubiti potsčalsja,

Proto vedut stydno v horu, Do Pilatovaho dvoru. 11. 

Čemu seho privodite? 

Jaku vinu prinosite? 

Carem sebe nazyvajet,

I narod naš prevrasčajet. 12. sem nebo užasesja, Sudij na sud privedesja, Nevinnosť Pilat uznajet,

Ruki svoi umyvajet.

SUS FALLS THE FIRS 

CROSS

3. 

JESUS FALLS UNDER THE WEIGHT OF THE CROSS. 

Under the weight of the Cross, or what is more, under the weight of my wickedness does my most sweet Jesus fall. What a grievous blow was to His Most Sacred 

Heart my first sin, the losing of my innocence! My Jesus, I would rather die than ever again cause 

Thee pain.

Mary, intercede for all the innocent souls for the mercy of endurance in all good, always, and for me, a sinner, ask for the grace of true sorrow.

Our Father: Hail, Mother of God: 

Suffering passion for us, Son of 

God, have mercy on me!

3. 

ISUS UPADET POD ŤAHA- ROM KRESTA. 

Slava strasťam Tvoim, slava dol hoterpiniju Tvojemu, Hospodi!

Pod faharom kresta, a čto boľše, pod ťaharom moich bezzakonij upadajet moj Isus najsolodšij. Jak ťažkim udarom dľa Jeho Najsvjatijšoho Serdca musi byti moj peršij upadok vo hrich, utrata mojej nevinnosti! Isuse, ja voľiju radše umerti, jak jesče nibuď koli Tebe obražati. 

Marije, vyprosi vsim nevinnym dušam lasku vyderžanija vo dobrom do konca, a mňi hrišnomu vyprosi lasku sčiroj pokuty.

Otče naš... Bohorodice Ďivo... 

Preterpivyj za nas strasti, Isuse 

Christe, Syne Božij, pomiluj mene!

13. 

Myslil Christa otpustiti, I Varravu pohubiti, 

No narod nemilostivyj,

Sud žaždet nespravedlivyj. 14. 

Obvinenna svoboždajet, Christa na smerť osuždajet, Rek sja Synom Boha byti, Proto dolžen jest umriti. 15. 

O Isuse vozžeľinnyj! 

Pročto's tak nenavidinnyj? 

Pro tvoja blaha velika, Pusča ťa mňat razbojnika. 16. 

Hor'ka kara nastupajet,

Bo zlosť ľudej neprestajet, Kažet Pilat obnažati, I pri stolpi l'uto biti.

JESUS MEETS HIS AFFLICTED MOTHER

4. 

JESUS MEETS HIS MOST HOLY MOTHER. 

Who can imagine the enormity of spiritual suffering, when these two most holy Persons met, and looked at one another, on the road leading toward Golgotha! In what awful way suffered then these two most holy Hearts for my grievous sins! Most Sacred 

Heart of Jesus, make me love Thee more and more! 

Most sweet Heart of Mary, be my patroness!

Our Father: Hail, Mother of God: 

Suffering passion for us, Son of 

God, have mercy on me!

4. 

ISUS VSTRIČAJET SVOJU PRESVJATU MATER'. 

Kto porozumijet velikosť duševnoho bolu Isusa i Marii, koli sii dvi najsvjaťijši Osoby stritilisja i popozerali na sebe vo dorozi na 

Golgothu! Jak strašno terpili tohdy sii dva najsvjaťijši Serdca za moich ťažkich provin! 

Serdce Isusa Najsvjaťijšoje, učini, čtoby ja lubil Tebe vsehda horjačijše! 

Najsolodše Serdce Marii, bud mojeju ochoronoju!

Otče naš... Bohorodice Ďivo... 

Preterpivyj za nas strasti, Isuse 

Christe, Syne Božij, pomiluj mene!

17. 

Rek Ty prorokov ustnami: 

Plesči moi dach na rany,

Sej deň javno ispolnisja, Duše hrišna užasisja! 18. 

Obnažennyj On vňi hrada, Boh Spas užasno postrada, Nit v Nem ot ľudskoj zlosti, Ot noh do hlavy cilosti. 19. O, pro koho terpiš sija, Christe pravdivyj Messija?

Terpiš pro naši zla dila, Naši slasti, prichoť tila. 20. 

O sich ubo umilimsja,

I pred krestom proslezimsja, Christe terpjaj za nas rany, Umiloserdisja na ny.

MON HELPS JESUS TO CARRYHIS CROSS

5. 

SIMON OF KÜREN HELPS JESUS BEAR HIS CROSS. 

My dear Jesus! Thou art close to death. O my soul, do you not wish to reveal to your Saviour your love and help in the bearing of His heavy Cross? 

Think that, if you do good to your neighbor, do this to Jesus, Himself, also! 

My Jesus, forgive those sins, with which I transgressed against the love of my neighbor. 

Mary, beseech for me that grace, that I may feel the misery of my neighbor and be ready to fly to his aid.

Our Father: Hail, Mother of God: 

Suffering passion for us, Son of 

God, have mercy on me!

5. 

SIMEON KIRINEJSKIJ PO- MAHAJET ISUSU NESTI KREST. 

Isuse milyj! 

Ty blizko smerti. Duše moja, ci ty ne chočeš zajaviti Spasiteľovi svojemu ľubov i pomosč Jemu nesti ťažkij krest?

Pamjataj, čto ty dobroho robiš svoim bližnym; se to čini i samomu Isusu! 

Isuse, prosti mi hrichi, jaki ja zrobil protiv ľubvi bližňoho. 

Marije, vyprosi mňi totu lasku, čtoby ja perečuvstvoval nedoľu bližnych i byl hotov nesti im pomosč.

Otče naš... Bohorodice Ďivo... 

Preterpivyj za nas strasti, Isuse 

Christe, Syne Božij, pomiluj mene!

O ISUSE PORANENNYJ. 

1. 

O Isuse poranennyj

I na kresti umorennyj, Ty mene tak miloval,

Sam sebe za mene dal. 2. 

Ty na kresti Krov' vycidil, 

Čtoby mene vysvobodil,

Svjatuju Krov' nevinnu, 

I to: pro moju vinu. 3. 

Ty pomer pročto? čtoby ja žil, Ty za mene dolh zaplatil, O Ty terpil nevinnyj, Proto, bo ja byl vinnyj. 4. 

O laskavyj Isuse moj!

Draha cina duši hrišnoj, Pomozi mňi chotiti 

Pro Tebe smert terpiti. 5. S Toboju ja choču žiti, Pro Tebe žizň položiti, Bo Ty drahij život Tvoj 

Položil za život moj.

VERONICA WIPES THE FACE OF JESUS

6.

VERONICA WIPES THE FACE OF JESUS. 

Lo, on my soul should rest my 

Saviour's wounded head and bloody face. O, for this grace I beseech 

Thee, my Jesus! Grant, my most merciful Saviour, that Thine head, most sacred and crowned with thorns, be ceaselessly in front of my eyes and especially during temptations.

Mary, much-suffering Mother, intercede for me for that mercy, that

I may always think of the sufferings of Jesus Christ, my Saviour.

Our Father: Hail, Mother of God: 

Suffering passion for us, Son of 

God, have mercy on me!

VERONIKA OBTIRAJET LICE ISUSA.

Daj mňi, najmiloserdňijšij Spasiteľu, čtoby pered očami mojej duši stojal vsehda obraz Tvojej najsvjaťijšoj, terňom uvinčannoj holovy; a peredovsim:

vo časi pokušenij.

Christa, Spasiteľa mojeho.

Ďivo... 

Preterpivyj za nas strasti, Isuse 

Christe, Syne Božij, pomiluj mene!

O BOŽE MOJ MILOSTIVYJ. 

1. 

O Bože moj milostivyj, Spasiteľu spravedlivyj,

Christe Spase na kresti visiši, Pro nas hrišnych ťažesť muk terpiši. 2. 

Pod krestom ľudije staňme, Na Isusa pozirajme;

Jak On hor'ko na duši i na tili Terpit muki ťažko ne po viňi. 3. 

On jak zlodij poimanyj, Motuzami povjazanyj, Ko Pilatu sudii vedennyj,

Na hor'ku smerť živo osuždennyj. 4. U Pilata prutovanyj, Ternovym vincem vinčanyj, Na smich pred Nim kolina shinajut, I po svjatych licach udarjajut.

JESUS-FALLS THE SECOND TIME

7.

JESUS FALLS UNDER THE WEIGHT OF THE CROSS THE SECOND TIME. 

The cause for the fall of Jesus is my pride and ceaseless sinning. 

O my soul, look under the Cross on the fallen Jesus and say: do you dare to hurt Him more? 

Much-suffering Mother, intercede for me, for the mercy of true humility and the endurance in goodness.

Our Father: Hail, Mother of God: 

Suffering passion for us, Son of 

God, have mercy on me!

ISUS DRUHIJ RAZ UPADAJET POD ŤAHAROM KRESTA.

Isusa upavšoho pod krestom i skaži, ci smiješ Jeho jesče daľše obražati? Mnohostradaľna 

Mati, vyprosi mňi lasku pravdivoj pokornosti i lasku vytervanija vo dobrom. 

Otče naš... Bohorodice Ďivo...

Preterpivyj za nas strasti, Isuse 

Christe, Syne Božij, pomiluj mene!

5. Odeždu Jemu snimajut, Trosť vo ruku polahajut, Carem Jeho smišno nazyvajut,

I vo počesť oči zaplevajut. 6. 

Vitaj carju! smichom kažut, Pokojny ruci povjažut, Kričat: raspni, o Pilate Jeho,

Na nas i d'itech našich Krov Jeho. 7. 

Pilat strašnyj sud iskazal, Do ruk hrišnych nevinnosť da!, Krest na svjaty pleči ťažkij otdal, Na nem i titlo viny napisal. 8. 

Uže Isus nese krest Svoj,

Hor'ko potit krovavyj znoj, Mati Jeho žalostno rydajet,

Ot bolizni serdca umľivajet.

JESUS SPEAKS TO THE WOMEN OF JERUSALEM

8. 

JESUS MEETS THE WEEPING WOMEN. 

My Jesus! Lo, I should weep for myself, for my numerous and imperfect sorrow for my sins. Bestow on me Thyself my Saviour, redeeming penitence; I shall accept this from Thy hands, that I may partake from the communion of 

Thy most sacred Blood! Punish me here, my Lord, but keep me for the eternal life. 

Much-suffering Mother, Mary, intercede for me that I may partake in the worthiness of thine most 

Holy Son, our Saviour, Jesus Christ.

Our Father: Hail, Mother of God: 

Suffering passion for us, Son of 

God, have mercy on me!

8. 

ISUS VSTRIČAJET PLAČUSČI 

NEVISTY. 

Isuse! Se ja povinen by plakati nad soboju po pričiny svoich premnohich a jesče neotpovidno spokutovannych hrichov! Ty sam,

Spasiteľu moj, naloži na mene spasiteľnuju pokutu; ja prijmu totu iz Tvoich ruk, čtoby mati učasť vo žertvi Tvojej Presvjatoj Krovi!

Tut mene, Hospodi, karaj, ale poderži mene na žitije vičnoje. 

Mnohostradaľna Mati Marije!

Vyprosi mňi učasť vo zasluhach 

Presvjatoj Krovi Tvojcho Syna, a 

Spasiteľa mojeho, Isusa Christa. 

Otče naš... Bohorodice Ďivo...

Christe, Syne Božij, pomiluj mene!

9. Rydajut nabožny ženy, Nit v sem poroka, ni viny, Bohobojny ľudije rydajut, A hrišnicy na to nevnimajut. 10. Isus padet, bo ťažkij krest Na plečach Jeho složen jesť, Polumertvyj tri razy na zemli 

Prostersja Boh i Stvoriteľ zemli. 11. 

Nit milosti, ni nepomožet 

Nikto bidnomu, choť možet,

Tokmo Simon selan Kirinejskij, Bohobojnyj muž i žiteľ seľskij. 12. 

Žalijet nad Toboj Bože, I krest Tvoj nesti pomože, Na horu Ťa Golgothu vyvede,

I na strašnyi muki privede.

JESUS FALLS THE THIRD TIME.

9.

JESUS FALLS FOR THE THIRD TIME. 

O, my most sweet Jesus, falling for the third time under the weight of the Cross. The reasons for this collapse are my sins and my leaning toward sins. My Jesus, help me that by resolving lasting betterment, in the midst of Thy heavy suffering, I may bring Thee comfort. 

Mary, shelter of sinners, save me from the occasion of sin and intercede for me that I may be steady in my good resolution.. 

Our Father: Hail, Mother of God: 

Suffering passion for us, Son of 

God, have mercy on me!

9. 

ISUS TRETIJ RAZ UPADAJET POD ŤAHAROM KRESTA. 

Ach, moj najsolodšij Isus padet tretij raz pod ťaharom kresta. 

Pričinoju seho padenija suť moi sohrišenija i moi hrišny naklonnosti. Isuse moj, dopomoži mňi, čtoby ja rišučim postanovlenijem popravlenija prines Tebi utišenije. vo Tvoich ťažkich terpinijach. 

Marije, pribižisče hrišnikov, sochrani mene ot nahody do hricha i uprosi mňi postojannosti vo dobrych postanovach. 

Otče naš... Bohorodice Ďivo...

Preterpivyj za nas strasti, Isuse 

Christe, Syne Božij, pomiluj mene!

13. 

Tam tri kresty zakopajut,

Na dvoch zbojnikov višajut, A posredi vozvyšajut tretij, I na sem jest Spasiteľ raspjatyj. 14.

Ahnec Božij neporočen, Visit na kresťi umučen,

Ruki, nohi probity hvozdami, I vse tilo objato ranami. 15.

Žaždet Ahnec umorennyj, Prosit piti vsesmirennyj, No i vody morja Sotvoriteľu, Nedovlijet svita Spasiteľu. 16.

Ale ocet želčem smišen, Žaždusčij v napoj prijem, Pokorno vkusil seho hor'kaho Napoja sebi v hubi dannaho.

JESU

10. 

JESUS'S CLOTHES ARE TORN OFF. 

What dreadful shame, what unspeakable pain to the Most Sacred 

Heart of our Saviour! Lo, He stands before the people with His robes torn off and they give Him to drink from gall and vinegar. 

My dearest Jesus, grant that I may with bitter tears weep over all my impure sins and my lack of self-control. Purest Mother of Virgin Mary and the my Saviour, Jesus 

Christ, cleanse my heart, body and soul.

Our Father: Hail, Mother of God: 

Suffering passion for us, Son of 

God, have mercy on me!

10.

IZ ISUSA ZOBĽIKAJUT ODEŽDU. 

Jakij strašnyj styd, jakij nevyskazannyj bol dľa Najsvjatijšoho Serdca Spasiteľa! Se On stoit pered narodom obdertyj z odeždy i Jemu podajut piti iz zolču i octu. 

Isuse najdorohšij, učiňi, čtoby ja hor'kimi slezami oplakoval vsi svoi nečisty hrichi i vsi svoi nevozderžnosti.

Neporočna Ďivo Marije i Mati 

Spasiteľa mojeho, Isusa Christa, očisti moje serdce, tilo i dušu. 

Otče naš... Bohorodice Ďivo...

Preterpivyj za nas strasti, Isuse 

Christe, Syne Božij, pomiluj mene!

17.

Hor'kij jesť to Carju napoj, V seredi žalosť i nepokoj, Mater' svoju v mukach viďiti, Ot žalosti serdca hor'ko mliti. 18. 

Točitsja krov' potokami 

Krestom, rukami, nohami,

A mati ju žalostno svirjajet, I s slezami svoimi mišajet. 19.

Uže vse jesť ispolnenno, Prorokami predrečenno, Isus Christos hlavu prekloňajet, Bohu Otcu Duch svoj otdajet. 20. 

Ne rydaj ty sama mati,

Jesť uže komu rydati, Nebo, zemľa, misjac, solnce Toho, Rydajut nevinno mučimaho.

INBI 

JESUS IS NAILED TO THE CROSS

11.

JESUS IS NAILED TO THE CROSS. 

My Jesus! My every grievous sin is a new nail, which I had hammered in Thy hands. Lamb of 

God covered with wounds, forgive me, that I had been the cause of 

Thy unspeakable suffering; for this, therefore, accept my burning thanks for Thy love and Thy sacrifice for me, a sinner. From now on my motto shall be: Rather death, than a sin! 

Mary, beseech for mercy that the Blood of Thy crucified Son may wash away from my soul all impurities.

Our Father: Hail, Mother of God: 

Suffering passion for us, Son of 

God, have mercy on me!

11. 

ISUSA PRIBIVAJUT NA KREST. 

Každyj moj ťažkij hrich, se jesť novyj hvozd, jakij ja vbil do 

Tvoich svjatych ruk. Isuse! Ahnče

Božij, pokrytyj ranami, prosti mňi, čto ja stal pričinoju Tvoich nevyskazajemych stradanij; ta i prijmi najhorjačuju poďaku za Tvoju

Tubov i požertvovanije za mene hrišnoho. Ot teper' moje heslo budet: radše smert, jak hrich! 

Marije, vyprosi mňi totu lasku, 

čtoby Krov Tvojeho razpjatoho 

Syna obmyla moju dušu so jej bezzakonijami.

Otče naš... Bohorodice Ďivo... 

Preterpivyj za nas strasti, Isuse 

Christe, Syne Božij, pomiluj mene!

21. 

Nebesa tma zaťahajet, Vsja zemľa sja kolybajet,

Solnca zarja smutno pohasajet, Misjac temna chmara pokryvajet. 22. 

Plačet cerkov davno zvana,

Premudrym Carem sozdanna, Zavisa zlata sja potrebila, I hodnosť jej už sja pominila. 23. 

A zemla trjasetsja strašno,

Hromy bijut, ryčat hlasno, Hroby temnyi sja otverzajut, Na strašnyj hrom mertvii Vozstavajut. 24. 

A skaly sja rozpadajut,

Zvizdy neba pohasajut, Strach i trepet zemľu obimajet, Bo na kresti Hospod umirajet.

JESUS DIES UPON THE CROSS

12.

JESUS IS DYING ON THE CROSS. 

Crucified Jesus, I believe that

Thou art the Son of the living 

God. Crucified Jesus, I hope that through Thy suffering and death,

I shall gain the forgiveness of my sins and the eternal life. Crucified 

Jesus, I love Thee, Who through love of me hast given Thyself to be crucified. 

Mary, accept me as Thy child and be with me in the hour of my death.

Our Father: Hail, Mother of God: 

Suffering passion for us, Son of 

God, have mercy on me!

ISUS UMIRAJET NA KRESŤI. 

Razpjatyj Isuse, ja viruju, čto

Ty jesi Synom Boha živaho. Raz pjatyj Isuse, ja upovaju, čto čerez zasluhi Tvoich muk i smerti ja dostanu prosčenije hrichov i život vičnyj. Razpjatyj Isuse, ja ľubľu

Tebe; Tebe, kto iz lubvi do mene dal pribiti Sebe do kresta. 

Marije, prijmi mene za ditinu tvoju i buď pri mňi VO hodiňi mojej smerti.

Otče naš... Bohorodice Ďivo... 

Preterpivyj za nas strasti, Isuse 

Christe, Syne Božij, pomiluj mene! 1 1

25.

Strašno vidiv, čto sja dijet, Na kolina ľudije padajut: Sej Božij Syn! smilo povidajut. 26. 

No hrišnika serdce ne krušit,

Bezbožnaho nič ne rušit, Mučitelej serdce zatverdilo, Čtoby oko sije ne vidilo. 27. 

Christiane! bižiť pod krest,

Na nem spasenije nam jesť, Christos na nem vas radi stradajet,

Vas i s Bohom Otcem pomirjajet. 28.

Prišel, i ta dasť Jeho ubiti!

JESUS IS TAKEN DOWN FROM THE CROSS

13.

JESUS IS TAKEN FROM THE CROSS. 

The most terrible sword of pain has pierced the heart of Mary, when in her lap rested the dead body of Jesus. O, how little have

I meditated on the sufferings of 

Jesus and Mary! 

O my Jesus, grant me the mercy so that I could feel with my whole heart with Thee and Thy longsuffering Mother! 

And thou, Mary, plant deeply into my heart the wounds of thy Son!

Our Father: Hail, Mother of God: 

Suffering passion for us, Son of 

God, have mercy on me!

Isusa i Marii!

SEPULCHRE

14. 

JESUS IS PLACED INTO THE SEPULCHRE. 

My Divine Saviour! Lo, I step up to Thine grave and prostrate, I kiss Thy five wounds. My Jesus, I am truly sorry for my sins, which had caused Thy death. My 

Jesus, grant Thy almighty mercy, that I may never again offend

Thee. 

Mary, Mother of God and my

Mother, intercede for me for the grace, that I may ever with a pure heart take to myself, at Holy Communion, the most precious Body and Blood of thine Son, as forfeit for eternal life.

Our Father: Hail, Mother of God: 

Suffering passion for us, Son of 

God, have mercy on me!

ISUSA VKLADAJUT DO 

HROBA. 

Božestvennyj moj Spasiteľu! Se ja vstuplu ko Tvojemu hrobu i padu na kolina i ciluju Tvoich pjať ran. Isuse, ja sčiro žaluju za svoi hrichi, jaki stali pričinoju 

Tvojej smerti. Učini, Isuse, svojeju vsemohusčoju laskoju, čtoby ja uže nikoli ne obražal Tebe.

Vo 

Marije, Mati Boža i Mati moja, vyprosi mňi totu lasku, čtoby ja vsehda čistym serdcem prinimal

Najsvjaťijšom Pričastiju Pre- Krov Tvojeho 

Syna, jako zavdatok na žitije vičnoje. svjatoje Tilo 

Otče naš... Bohorodice Ďivo...

Preterpivyj za nas strasti, Isuse 

Christe, Syne Božij, pomiluj mene!

CONCLUDING PRAYER. 

O my blessed and gracious God! 

I give Thee thanks that Thou hast allowed me to complete this devotion of the Way of the Cross. 

However, do not let my unworthiness be in the way of the sacrifice of Thy great mercies. Look on the face of Thy crucified Son and accept His most precious Blood and tears; have mercy on me, and on every sinner, too.

Grant that the memory of the agony and patience of my most sweet Jesus, as well as the suffering of the most pure Mother, would be deeply engraved on my heart, and that these would give me strength, too, in my daily endurance; that they may give me a wish for the doing of good deeds, and that through our Lord, Jesus Christ, may lead me to the eternal life. 

Our Father: Hail, Mother of God: 

Glory be: Now and ever: O my sweetest Jesus, do not be my Judge, but my Saviour! AMEN.

MOLITVA ZAKĽUČITEĽNA. 

O blahij i milostivyj Bože! Blahodarju Tebi, čto dozvolil jesi mňi dokončiti siju pobožnosť Krestnoj 

Dorohi. Ne dopusti, čtoby moje nedostoinstvo bylo pričinoju polučenija Tvoich velikich milostej.

Pohľaň na lice Tvojeho razpjatoho 

Syna, i prijmi Jeho dorohocinnu krov ta i slezy, i imij miloserdije nad mnoju i nad vsimi hrišnikami. 

Daj, čtoby pamjať strastej i terpinija mojeho Sladčajšoho Spasiteľa i stradanija Jeho Prečistoj Materi byli hluboko zaskiplenny vo mojem serdci i ukripili mene VO každodennych terpinijach, i zaochoťili mene do dobrych dil, i čtoby provadili mene do vičnoho života čerez Hospoda našeho Isusa Christa.

Otče naš: Bohorodice Divo: Slava Otcu: I nyňi: O Sladčajšij Isuse moj, zmiloserdisja nad mnoju, i ne buď mňi sudijeju, ale Spasitelem. AMIN!

O Marije, presvjataja, Divo prečistaja.

Pisǎ 8. 

Jehda na strast hotovilsja Isuse, Vo zahradi Getsimanskoj Isuse,

Ďiva prečista, Mati bolezna, 

Žalostno plakala. 

Spusčal jesi pot krovavyj Isuse, Zemľu slezy oblivavyj Isuse, Diva prečista, Mati bolezna, 

Žalostno plakala.

Pisni Pisň k Otcu Nikolaju. 

O kto, kto Nikolaja lubit,

O kto, kto Nikolaju služit, Tomu svjatyj Nikolaj, Na vsjakij čas pomahaj: 

Nikolaj, Nikolaj!

O kto, kto živet v jeho dvori, Pomosčnik na zemli i morji,

Izmet jeho ot napasti, Ne dast jemu v hrichi vpasti: 

Nikolaj, Nikolaj!

O kto, kto k nemu pribihajet, Na pomosč jeho prizyvajet,

Toj ne budet ošukannyj, I hrichovnoj ujdet rany: 

Nikolaj, Nikolaj! 

Nikolaj ty molisja za ny, Prosim ta Otče so slezami, My ta budem voschvalati, Imja tvoje veličati:

Vo viki, vo viki!

Pisni 

283 Papskij Himn. 

Hde Petrova Svjata mohila, Tam zvučit molitva mila. Iz ust tysjač i tysjačej, Himn različnych rodov ľudej: 

Bože! Chraň Svjataho Otca, Christovoho namistnika.
